Its performance is evaluated on a synthetic refrigerator dataset comprising 27 simulated refrigerators with varying sensor configurations, operating conditions, and failure types, providing a controlle

</details>


### [241] [Dueling World Models: Advantage-Style Action Channels for Common-Mode Distractor Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06706)
*Jiazhuo Li,Yiming Fei,Zhiruo Zhou,Heikichi Hayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种极简方法以解决潜在世界模型中的"动作盲"问题：通过类似dueling分解的技巧，在潜在动力学中减去动作上的均值效应，从而消除与动作无关的干扰因素（如不可控的运动），恢复干净的可控通道。该方法无需奖励、重建或辅助损失，可直接应用于任何行动条件世界模型（包括冻结的预训练模型），并在网格世界、合成生成器、连续控制和Atari等多个环境中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型在场景中存在不可控运动时会出现"动作盲"问题——不同动作的预测变得难以区分，尽管训练损失仍在下降。现有解决方案（重建、任务奖励、辅助目标）都增加了额外的机制或假设，作者希望找到一种极简替代方案。

Method: 借鉴强化学习中dueling分解的思想（将价值分解为状态基线和动作优势），在潜在动力学中减去预测在所有动作上的均值效应。因为干扰因素（动作无关的变化）存在于各动作共享的部分中，减去均值即可将其消除，仅保留与动作相关的可控通道。该操作仅发生在读取时，因此可无修改地应用于任何行动条件世界模型。

Result: 在网格世界、合成生成器、连续控制、Atari等环境中，隔离出的通道成功恢复了智能体自身的影响，而纠缠的预测器则失败；干扰泄漏与零不可区分。事后应用该方法还能在现成模型中浮现出原始输出无法捕获的动作通道，并在网格世界中转化为目标达成控制。作者还证明了在离散和采样动作集中有限样本下的精确消除。

Conclusion: 作者证明了一个极简的减法操作足以解决潜在世界模型的动作盲问题，无需任何额外奖励、重建或辅助损失。该方法具有广泛适用性（可应用于冻结的预训练模型），并给出了其适用边界——即干扰因素的运动与动作相关时该方法失效。

Abstract: Latent world models plan by predicting future states from an action, but when a scene contains motion the agent does not control, they quietly go action-blind: predictions for different actions become indistinguishable even as the training loss keeps improving. Existing remedies suppress this distraction with reconstruction, task reward, or auxiliary objectives, each adding machinery or assumptions. We show that a minimal alternative suffices, borrowed from the dueling decomposition of value into a state baseline and an action advantage: in latent dynamics, subtracting a prediction's mean effect over actions cancels whatever the actions share--the action-independent variation where distractors live--leaving a clean, controllable channel, with no reward, no reconstruction, and no distractor-specific auxiliary loss. Because this is only a subtraction at readout time, it applies unchanged to any action-conditioned world model, including frozen pretrained ones. Across a gridworld, synthetic generators with known factors, distracting continuous control, and natural-pixel Atari, the isolated channel recovers the agent's own effect where entangled predictors fail, with nuisance leak indistinguishable from zero; applied post hoc it surfaces an action channel in off-the-shelf models that their raw readouts miss, and it converts into goal-reaching control in the gridworld. We prove the cancellation is exact in finite samples for both discrete and sampled action sets, and we state its measured boundary--distractors whose motion tracks the action--together with the remaining limitations in the appendix.

</details>


### [242] [Multi-Level Modeling of Large Language Model Inference Latency and Energy via Hybrid Analytical--Machine-Learning Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06723)
*Saeid Shokoufa,Mohammad Erfan Sadeghi,Mehdi Kamal,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HYMELL，一个混合三层框架，结合分析建模与机器学习来估计LLM推理的延迟和能耗，在NVIDIA H100上对LLaMA 3 8B实现小于5%的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM规模快速扩展导致计算成本、能耗和推理延迟显著增加，需要准确的估计方法以支持可持续AI部署和硬件感知设计。

Method: HYMELL采用三层混合框架：(1) 分析建模估计底层原语操作；(2) 机器学习预测高层组件；(3) 端到端模型捕获系统级开销，覆盖prefill和decode两个阶段，支持密集和MoE架构、MHA和GQA注意力机制。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100 GPU上取得高预测精度，对LLaMA 3 8B在prefill和decode阶段误差均小于5%。

Conclusion: HYMELL能够直接从架构参数预测执行成本，实现快速、无需硬件的设计空间探索和能效优化。

Abstract: The rapid scaling of Large Language Models (LLMs) has significantly increased computational cost, energy consumption, and inference latency, making accurate estimation essential for sustainable artificial intelligence deployment and hardware-aware design. In this work, we introduce Hybrid Modeling for Energy and Latency of LLMs (HYMELL), a hybrid three-level framework for estimating LLM inference latency and energy by combining analytical modeling with machine learning (ML). HYMELL models LLM execution through a three-level hierarchy: analytical estimation of primitive operations, ML prediction of higher-level components, and an end-to-end model that captures system-level overheads across both prefill and decode phases. The framework supports diverse architectures, including dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) feed-forward networks (FFNs), as well as multi-head attention (MHA) and grouped-query attention (GQA) mechanisms. Evaluated on an NVIDIA H100 graphics processing unit (GPU), HYMELL achieves high predictive accuracy; notably, for LLaMA 3 8B, it attains less than 5% error for both prefill and decode phases. By predicting execution costs directly from architectural parameters, it enables fast, hardware-free design space exploration and energy-efficient optimization.

</details>


### [243] [Solver-Guided Reasoning for Mixed-Equilibrium Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06741)
*Han Wang,Philippe Beardsell,Boning Li,Aaron Sasmita,Shuai Li,Hongyuan Zha,Baoxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出混合策略决策树（MDT），利用求解器输出而非人类数据来引导LLM进行博弈中的均衡推理，在无限注德州扑克中将ℓ1距离减少52.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的推理通常依赖人类文本和演示，但在复杂博弈中人类行为常偏离均衡（尤其在混合策略均衡中偏向纯策略），导致LLM博弈策略薄弱。

Method: 提出混合策略决策树（MDT），将均衡中的隐性最优性转化为稀疏的策略规则，使人类和LLM都能理解；使用求解器输出而非人工标注，可扩展到任意新状态和延续。作者查询了超过2.5亿个混合策略决策作为数据。

Result: 在8种不同LLM配置下，MDT结合其他技术将到均衡的ℓ1距离减少52.6%；消融实验验证了各组件贡献，并在River-endgame和Liar's Dice实验中验证了策略忠实度和可移植性。

Conclusion: 使用求解器输出替代人类数据可以有效提升LLM在游戏中的均衡推理能力，MDT作为可理解的策略规则表示能显著改善LLM的博弈表现，且该方法具有超越原始场景的可移植性。

Abstract: Reasoning in large language models (LLMs) is often grounded in human text, human demonstrations, and human-generated rationales. For equilibrium reasoning in complex games, however, relying on human data can be suboptimal. In fact, human play is often guided by intuition and heuristics and can deviate substantially from game equilibrium. This discrepancy is amplified in games with mixed-strategy equilibria, where human data is heavily biased toward pure strategies. Consequently, conditioning LLMs on this data yields weak game strategies. To grant LLMs the reasoning capacity in games, in this work, we study how to elicit equilibrium play using solver output. We propose Mixed-Strategy Decision Tree (MDT), which articulates the silent optimality of the equilibrium into sparse strategic rules that both humans and LLMs could understand. Using solver output rather than human annotation allows us to extend the input to arbitrarily new states and continuations. We instantiate this study on No-Limit Texas Hold'em by querying a solver oracle for over \textbf{250 million mixed-strategy decisions}; MDT together with other techniques \textbf{reduces the $\ell_1$ distance to the equilibrium by $52.6\%$} across $8$ different LLM configurations. A Route-only ablation tests the incremental contribution of the shadow-based contrast, while complete River-endgame and Liar's Dice experiments evaluate strategic fidelity and portability beyond the original NLH communication setting.

</details>


### [244] [KReF: Training-Free Retrieval for Long-Term Time-Series Forecasting and Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06748)
*Yang Zhang,Rui Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KReF是一种无需训练的检索框架，通过检索历史相似的lookback-future对来构建查询点附近的经验预测分布，在六个LTSF基准和四个预测范围上取得了最低的CRPS分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概率性长期时间序列预测依赖训练模型，而无需训练的保形方法通常只能围绕预有点预测器构建区间，无法原生表示完整的预测分布；顺序变体在长预测范围上还存在反馈延迟问题。

Method: KReF采用训练无关的检索框架：对每个lookback序列进行稳健预处理后，使用手工统计量或冻结的随机傅里叶特征进行嵌入，检索相似的历史lookback-future对；利用相似度权重直接定义预测质量、分位数、CRPS和加权均值点预测；同时利用查询lookback构建概率积分变换映射，并应用验证集选择的扩展和收缩率来调整区间边界。

Result: 在六个LTSF基准和四个预测范围上，KReF在所有12个数据集-嵌入设置中获得最低CRPS，在9个设置中获得最低IS90；无需梯度拟合的点预测在两个数据集上达到或超过训练基线；存档-预言家分析显示在更细粒度的范围和通道路由下仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: 检索是一种有用且未被充分探索的长期时间序列预测归纳偏置，KReF证明了无需训练的检索方法在概率性LTSF任务上的竞争力。

Abstract: Probabilistic long-term time-series forecasting commonly relies on trained models. Training-free conformal methods typically construct intervals around a pre-existing point forecaster and do not natively represent a complete predictive distribution; sequential variants additionally suffer from increasingly delayed feedback at long horizons. We propose KReF, a training-free retrieval framework that treats retrieved historical futures as a querylocal empirical predictive distribution. After robust preprocessing, KReF embeds each lookback using handcrafted statistics or frozen random Fourier features and retrieves similar historical lookback-future pairs. Their similarity weights directly define predictive masses, quantiles, CRPS, and a weighted-mean point forecast. KReF further uses the observed query lookback to construct a probability-integral-transform map and applies validation-selected expansion and shrinkage rates to adapt interval boundaries. Across six LTSF benchmarks and four horizons, KReF obtains the lowest CRPS in all 12 dataset-embedding settings and the lowest IS90 in 9 settings. Without gradient-based fitting, its point forecasts also match or surpass trained baselines on two of six datasets. An archive-oracle analysis further reveals substantial headroom under finer horizon- and channel-wise routing. These results establish retrieval as a useful and underexplored inductive bias for LTSF.

</details>


### [245] [Sub-Quadratic Bisimulation Metrics via Approximate Nearest Neighbors: Coverage-Augmented Guarantees and Computable Two-Sided Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06762)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Joyanta Jyoti Mondal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证书的亚二次复杂度双模拟度量计算方法，通过近似最近邻索引选择更新对，并利用单调上下界包装精确度量，实现比传统二次方法更高效的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的双模拟度量计算需要对每个状态对进行Wasserstein固定点迭代更新，产生二次方的成对计算开销，在大规模MDP中难以承受。

Method: 提出证书承载的亚二次方法：(1) 利用近似最近邻索引选择由精确受限算子更新的状态对；(2) 单调下界和上界运行在每个扫描中包围精确度量；(3) 提供覆盖增强的任意时间界限，算法返回可观测的夹层宽度作为证书，上下聚类一致性验证覆盖聚合的精确恢复。

Result: 极限误差至多为max(ρ, ε/(1-γ))，实验验证了恒等式和包络性质。时序实验确认了二次与亚二次的扩展差异，在64状态基准上精确受限细化达到精确度量天际线，而MICo和DBC基线高出22-33倍。

Conclusion: 亚二次双模拟度量计算是可行的，但覆盖率项无法消除，需通过证书机制保证结果可信度，且在实际任务中能显著超越奖励仅度量的方法。

Abstract: Bisimulation metrics quantify behavioral similarity in Markov decision processes, but their Wasserstein fixed-point operator updates every state pair and incurs quadratic pairwise work. We give a certificate-carrying sub-quadratic method for MDPs with bounded transition support and a useful low-dimensional indexing representation: an approximate-nearest-neighbor index selects the pairs updated by the exact restricted operator, while monotone lower and upper runs enclose the exact metric at every sweep. The main analytical result is a coverage-augmented anytime bound: local index quality alone cannot control global error, because uncovered pairs retain their initialization gap. The limiting error is at most $\max(ρ,\eop/(1-γ))$, and with exact covered backups the lower arm satisfies $\|\dann-d\|_\infty=ρ$. Because $ρ$ depends on the unknown exact metric, the algorithm returns the observable sandwich width instead; agreement of the induced lower and upper clusterings certifies exact recovery of the covered aggregation. A reward-oblivious lower bound shows sub-quadratic index-first coverage cannot remove the coverage term, while a separate adaptive lower bound requires $Ω(|\Scal|)$ pair evaluations. Exact-operator experiments verify the identity and enclosure in every seeded run, and timing experiments recover quadratic versus sub-quadratic scaling under both cheap and full Wasserstein backups. On the grouped $|\Scal|=64$ benchmark, exact restricted refinement reaches the exact-metric skyline once retrieval covers roughly half of all pairs, while independently trained MICo and DBC baselines stay $22$-$33\times$ above that skyline at every retrieval budget. Taxi shows the certificate abstaining under an uninformative embedding, while a $2500$-state gridworld improves over a reward-only metric by $28.6\%$ using $12.8\%$ of one quadratic sweep.

</details>


### [246] [CubicQuant: Parametric Non-Uniform Codebooks for High-Throughput LLM Inference with 1-8-Bit Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06763)
*Xuetian Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CubicQuant，一种基于三次曲线映射的参数化非均匀标量量化格式，用于大语言模型推理中的权重量化。它通过两个形状参数和一个尺度参数将均匀间隔的幅度码映射到非均匀重建层级，支持1-8位权重，且在G=128时相比均匀整数量化在RMSE上取得显著降低，并可在GPU上直接执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理中的权重量化需要在自适应重建层级与适合GPU高效执行的规则表示之间取得平衡。现有方法中，均匀整数量化表达力有限，低比特浮点格式结构固定，学习式码本虽灵活但解码不规则且附带额外开销。

Method: CubicQuant引入一种参数化非均匀标量量化格式：通过单调三次曲线（由两个形状参数和一个尺度参数定义）将均匀间隔的幅度码映射到非均匀重建层级。该方法保留了密集整数码流，在每个权重组内自适应调整重建层级，包含对称均匀整数量化作为精确特例，有效位宽为B+64/G比特。作者推导了均匀、高斯和拉普拉斯分布下的总体失真，提出了连续和Dynamic-A8兼顾的拟合目标，并描述了直接的打包权重GPU执行方案。

Result: 在G=128、每分布15,360个样本的实验中，W4 CubicQuant相比最优裁剪的四比特均匀整数量化，在均匀、高斯和拉普拉斯分布上分别降低了3.90%、13.49%和28.14%的重建RMSE。相比最优枚举的四比特有限浮点格式分别降低3.90%、9.44%和6.27%。H200内核初步测量显示工作负载相关的性能交叉点：窄GEMV下模型dtype执行更快，行数增长时Dynamic A8更有利。

Conclusion: CubicQuant展示了非均匀参数量化格式在表示能力和直接可执行性方面的潜力，但下游模型质量和跨设备端到端性能仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Weight quantization for large-language-model inference must balance adaptive reconstruction levels with representations regular enough for efficient GPU execution. Uniform integers constrain each group to a linear grid. Low-bit floating-point formats use a fixed exponent-mantissa structure, while learned codebooks gain flexibility at the cost of irregular decoding and additional metadata.
  We introduce CubicQuant, a parametric non-uniform scalar format that preserves a dense integer code stream while adapting reconstruction levels within each weight group. A monotonic cubic curve, specified by two shape parameters and one scale, maps uniformly spaced magnitude codes to non-uniform levels. The family spans 1-8-bit weight payloads, contains symmetric uniform integer quantization as an exact special case, and has effective width B + 64/G bits per weight for payload width B and group size G. We derive population distortion under Uniform, Gaussian, and Laplace distributions, formulate continuous and Dynamic-A8-carrier-aware fitting objectives, and describe direct packed-weight GPU execution.
  For finite groups of G=128 with 15,360 samples per distribution, W4 CubicQuant reduced reconstruction RMSE relative to optimally clipped four-bit uniform integer quantization by 3.90% on Uniform, 13.49% on Gaussian, and 28.14% on Laplace samples. Relative to the best enumerated four-bit finite floating-point format, the reductions were 3.90%, 9.44%, and 6.27%. Preliminary H200 kernel measurements show a workload-dependent crossover: model-dtype execution is faster for narrow GEMV, while Dynamic A8 becomes favorable as row count grows. The results establish the format's representational promise and direct executability; downstream model quality and cross-device end-to-end performance remain open evaluation questions.

</details>


### [247] [Hidden Gauge Controls Feature Specialization in ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06766)
*Tongxi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究过参数化ReLU网络中特征所有权（feature ownership）的可控性问题，证明在保持初始预测函数完全不变的情况下，仅通过改变正齐次缩放规范（scaling gauge）就能决定哪个神经元最终习得教师特征，并产生Θ(D²)量级的专业化时间分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解过参数化网络中多个功能重复的神经元如何被分配任务相关结构，以及初始预测器不可见的参数选择是否能够控制特征学习的归属和速度。

Method: 在高斯教师-学生模型中，固定完整初始函数，仅变化正齐次缩放规范；通过反应-传输分解（reaction-transport decomposition）分析特征系数和方向的不同迁移率；使用精确的数学证明结合小步全批次梯度下降验证。

Result: 相反的规范产生不同的特征轨迹和Θ(D²)的专业化时间分离；有利规范确定性地选择某个神经元成为特征所有者并将其他神经元的功能贡献归零；证明了全局选择和功能性剪枝，并在总体和有限样本训练中验证了损失、对齐、剪枝和耗散轨迹。

Conclusion: 初始预测器既不能决定特征何时被学习，也不能决定哪个神经元学习该特征——这一结论挑战了对过参数化网络可解释性的传统认知。

Abstract: Training changes a network's predictions while allocating task-relevant structure across its internal units. In an overparameterized ReLU network, several neurons can begin with exactly the same functional role, yet one may acquire a teacher feature while the others become redundant. We call the identity of that neuron feature ownership and ask whether it can be controlled by a parameter choice invisible to the initial predictor. In a tractable Gaussian teacher--student model, we fix the complete initial function and vary only a positive-homogeneous scaling gauge. Opposite gauges produce distinct feature trajectories and a sharp $Θ(D^2)$ separation in specialization time that no global change of clock can explain. Among any fixed number of initially duplicate students, assigning the favorable gauge to one neuron deterministically selects it as the owner and drives the remaining functional contribution to zero. An exact reaction--transport decomposition attributes the effect to different mobilities for changing a feature's coefficient and direction. We prove global selection and functional pruning, extend finite-time selection to visible perturbations and small-step full-batch gradient descent, and verify the predicted loss, alignment, pruning, and dissipation trajectories in population and finite-sample training. The initial predictor therefore determines neither when the feature is learned nor which neuron learns it.

</details>


### [248] [ArchEGraph: A Large-Scale Graph Dataset for Geometry-Topology-Physics Aligned Building Energy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06772)
*Yihui Li,Yihui Chen,Kaidi Zha,Xiaoyue Yan,Zhexuan Yu,Shiqi Dai,Jun Xiao,Jun Yin,Ramon Elias Weber,Borong Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ArchEGraph，一个大规模建筑能量建模基准数据集，将建筑表示为包含几何、拓扑、天气和区域级热负荷的异构图，涵盖5,481栋建筑和49,326个建筑-天气仿真案例，并定义了两个基准任务：多边形网格图重建和拓扑感知负荷预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑能量使用的准确估计对于实现碳中和至关重要，但缺乏将建筑几何显式映射到性能的大规模数据集，限制了机器学习模型对建筑设计的快速反馈支持。

Method: 将建筑表示为异构图（包含几何、拓扑、天气和区域级热负荷），构建包含5,481栋建筑、49,326个仿真案例的数据集，并定义图重建和负荷预测两个基准任务及标准化评估协议。

Result: 数据集包含超过133,000个空间节点和144万个面节点，并进行了跨建筑和跨气候的泛化实验，验证了模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ArchEGraph为研究建筑能量建模中几何-拓扑-物理耦合提供了统一测试平台，支持可扩展和通用代理模型的开发与评估。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of building energy use is essential for achieving carbon neutral and sustainable buildings. To better understand the influence of design decisions on building energy use and calibrate machine learning models that can give architects and engineers rapid design feedback, large-scale datasets are needed that explicitly map building geometry to performance. We present ArchEGraph, a large-scale benchmark dataset that represents buildings as heterogeneous graphs with aligned geometry, topology, weather, and zone-level thermal loads. The dataset contains 5,481 buildings and 49,326 validated building-weather simulation cases. In total, it includes over 133,000 space nodes and 1.44 million face nodes, reflecting substantial geometric and topological complexity. Based on ArchEGraph, we define two benchmark tasks: (i) graph reconstruction from polygonal meshes, aiming to recover topological structure from geometric representations; and (ii) topology-informed load prediction, which leverages graph structure and temporal weather conditions to forecast zone-level response time series. We further introduce standardized evaluation protocols for both tasks and conduct cross-building and cross-climate generalization experiments to assess model robustness. ArchEGraph provides a unified testbed for studying geometry-topology-physics coupling in building energy modeling, enabling the development and evaluation of scalable and generalizable surrogate models.

</details>


### [249] [Faster Query-Key Learning Sharpens Attention in Self-Attention Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06776)
*Rahul Vashisht,Harish G. Ramaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了单层自注意力模型中查询-键电路和输出-值电路的参数化方式如何影响注意力模式，发现分解参数化会导致两个电路学习率的隐式重缩放，更快的查询-键学习产生更尖锐的注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自注意力层中不同电路（查询-键与输出-值）的参数化方式如何影响注意力分布和训练动态，从而更好地解释和控制注意力模式。

Method: 通过梯度流分析推导闭式动态，证明分解参数化隐式地重缩放两个电路的学习率，参数沿直线移动，相对速度由学习率决定；结合实验验证。

Result: 查询-键学习相对更快时，模型通过增加相关令牌的注意力质量来补偿较慢的输出-值学习，从而产生更尖锐的注意力；在保持预测性能的同时改善了注意力可解释性。

Conclusion: 两个电路相对学习率的差异是控制注意力集中度的关键因素，这为设计具有更好可解释性的注意力模型提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A standard self-attention layer consists of two interacting circuits: the query-key circuit that governs attention allocation, and the output-value circuit that maps attended representations to predictions. Collapsed and factorized parameterizations of the query-key and output-value circuits lead to qualitatively different attention patterns. In particular, some parameterizations give sharper attention to task-relevant tokens, at a similar training loss. We analyze how the parameterizations of these circuits shape the parameter trajectories in single-layer self-attention models trained for next-token prediction. Through gradient-flow analysis, we show that factorization induces implicit rescaling of the two circuits' learning rates. We derive closed-form dynamics showing that output-value and query-key parameters move along a line, with relative speeds determined by their learning rates. Faster query-key learning relative to output-value learning thus produces sharper attention, as the model compensates for slower output-value learning by increasing attention mass on relevant tokens. Experiments show that differences in the relative learning rates of the two circuits govern attention concentration. This improves attention interpretability proxies while maintaining comparable predictive performance.

</details>


### [250] [Understanding Differentiable Embeddings Through Differential and Integral Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06809)
*Xinyu Zhang,Klaus Mueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的几何框架，用以理解和诊断非线性降维嵌入的可信度，将投影字形、映射连续性和基于传输的分析统一为单一几何对象，并提供微分与积分两个互补视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非线性降维嵌入诊断方法（投影字形、映射连续性评分、基于传输的分析）彼此独立、缺乏统一框架，难以理解它们何时一致或冲突，也无法提供完整的可信度评估。

Method: 通过证明所有现有诊断方法都源自每个可微嵌入诱导的单一几何对象，构建涵盖微分视角（一阶项恢复投影字形、二阶曲率量化线性近似可靠性）和积分视角（沿高维路径追踪几何）的统一框架，并证明映射连续性是其他分析的前提条件，同时证明积分视角不可约简。

Result: 实验在合成与真实数据集上验证了理论预测，展示了基于曲率的可信度估计在单细胞嵌入上的准确性，并表明积分分析能够区分单值嵌入与路径依赖的优化嵌入，这是现有逐点诊断方法无法做到的。

Conclusion: 该框架在嵌入几何推导的诊断方面具有理论完备性，积分视角不可由任何局部测量（任意点、任意导阶）复现，为非线性降维嵌入的可信度评估提供了统一而完整的理论基础。

Abstract: How can an analyst decide whether a nonlinear dimensionality reduction embedding can be trusted? Existing diagnostics provide only partial answers: projection glyphs characterize local sensitivity, map-continuity scores measure local conditioning, and transport-based analyses reveal path-dependent inconsistencies. However, these methods appear unrelated and provide no common framework for understanding when they agree or not. We show that they are all derived from a single geometric object induced by every differentiable embedding, whether defined implicitly through optimization or explicitly by a learned mapping. This framework provides two complementary geometric views of an embedding. The differential view explains local behavior: its first-order term recovers projection glyphs, while its second-order curvature quantifies how far their linear approximation remains reliable. The integral view follows the same geometry along high dimensional paths and determines whether an embedding depends only on the current state or also on the path taken to reach it. We further show that map-continuity is a prerequisite for the other analyses. The framework is theoretically complete for diagnostics derived from the embedding geometry, and we prove the integral view irreducible: no amount of local measurement at any number of points, to any order of derivative, reproduces what it detects. Classical rank-based metrics form a complementary class based on finite-scale neighborhood relationships. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets validate theoretical predictions, demonstrate accurate curvature-based trust estimates on single-cell embeddings, and show that the integral analysis distinguishes single-valued embeddings from path-dependent optimization-based embeddings in ways that existing pointwise diagnostics cannot.

</details>


### [251] [Multiscale Reward Hedging from Correct Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06825)
*Pahan Dewasurendra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了从正确演示中学习的问题，提出了一种基于容忍最优性测试和共享投票的奖励对冲方法，首次为非连续奖励类提供了无时域（horizon-free）的保证。该方法在连续类上实现了度量熵积分界，多项式熵下总间隔为O(d log A)，并在有限线性上下文推荐中达到O(d)遗憾，同时给出了下界和多种推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从正确演示中学习比监督学习更困难，因为学习者预测后只能看到一个有效的答案，而无法知道自己的答案是否有效，也观察不到任何奖励。已有奖励对冲保证仅适用于有限奖励类，缺乏对连续类别的无时域保证。

Method: 核心思路是在每个精度尺度上共享一次对容忍最优性测试的投票。目标奖励在每个尺度上有一个幸存的代理，当预测的间隔超过该尺度时，代理翻倍。这产生了同时尾界，并通过积分尾界限定累积隐藏间隔，由度量熵积分控制。

Result: 多项式熵(A/ε)^d下总间隔为O(d log A)，统计速率为O(d/m)。有界线性上下文推荐达到O(d)遗憾（任意紧致菜单），固定半径秩二推荐时间复杂度为O(KT^2)。还证明了Ω(d)下界、低秩和ReLU网络推论，以及引入演示者累积次优性的鲁棒定理。MovieLens审计在1.7秒内完成并改善了平均潜在间隔。

Conclusion: 该研究首次为连续奖励类提供了无时域的奖励对冲保证，虽然一般投票计算可能昂贵，但在单维Lipschitz参数曲线和固定半径秩二推荐场景下可在多项式时间内实现。通过适当的非正确预测，实现了对任意紧致菜单的首次多项式有限界。

Abstract: Learning from correct demonstrations is harder than supervised learning when many answers are correct: after predicting, the learner sees one valid answer but not whether its own answer was valid, nor any reward. Existing reward-hedging guarantees consequently assume a finite reward class. We give the first horizon-free guarantee for continuous classes. The key is to hedge in one shared vote over tolerant optimality tests at every accuracy scale. A target reward has one surviving proxy per scale, and a prediction with gap above that scale doubles the proxy. This yields the simultaneous tail bound $|\{t:\ell_t>2^{-j}\}|\leq \log_2\mathcal N(\mathcal G,2^{-j-1})+j$, where $\mathcal G$ is the class of optimality-gap functions. Integrating the tails gives cumulative hidden gap bounded by a metric-entropy integral, independently of the number of rounds. Polynomial entropy $(A/ε)^d$ gives $O(d\log A)$ total gap and a fast $O(d/m)$ statistical rate. For bounded linear contextual recommendation, the result is $O(d)$ regret for arbitrary compact menus. This is the first polynomial finite bound without structural restrictions on the menus, at the price of improper prediction. Although the general vote can be expensive, it is exactly polynomial-time for one-dimensional Lipschitz parameter curves. Fixed-radius rank-two recommendation takes $O(KT^2)$ time for menus of size $K$. We also prove an $Ω(d)$ lower bound, low-rank and bounded ReLU-network corollaries, and a robust theorem that adds only the demonstrator's cumulative suboptimality. A reproducible adaptive stress test illustrates the predicted scale adaptation. After factorization, an exact MovieLens audit runs in 1.7 CPU seconds across ten users and improves mean latent gap over both a demonstrated-rating policy and a proper online baseline. The learner uses only action demonstrations and never observes a reward or a loss.

</details>


### [252] [Graph Machine: Exploring Edge Mechanisms as an Inductive Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06834)
*Lintai Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph Machine架构，通过边增强注意力和边中心引荐两种显式边机制，为Transformer引入更强的迭代遍历潜在关系的归纳偏置，在数独任务上优于Transformer基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer擅长全局基于内容的匹配，但推理问题可能受益于更强的迭代遍历潜在关系的归纳偏置，现有架构缺乏显式的边机制来建模节点间关系。

Method: 提出Graph Machine架构，包含两种显式边机制：(1)边增强注意力（Edge-augmented attention），边调制节点间的注意力；(2)边中心引荐（Edge-centric referral），节点交换地址以更新边。模型能够在不同层间动态且可微地构建和修正关系图。

Result: 在受控的数独任务设置下，Graph Machine优于Transformer基线；消融研究和机制分析表明性能提升归因于边机制。模型还意外发现了数独几何的紧凑基于边的构造方式。

Conclusion: 显式边机制是一种有前景的架构设计，值得更广泛的评估和应用。

Abstract: Transformers provide a powerful architecture for global content-based matching, but reasoning problems may benefit from a stronger inductive bias toward iterative traversal of latent relations. We introduce Graph Machine, an architecture with two explicit edge-based mechanisms: Edge-augmented attention, in which edges modulate attention between nodes, and edge-centric referral, in which nodes exchange addresses to update their edges. Conceptually, this enables the model to dynamically and differentiably construct and revise relational graphs across layers. We study this inductive bias using Sudoku under controlled settings and find that Graph Machine outperforms Transformer baselines, with ablation studies and mechanistic analysis attributing the gains to the edge mechanisms. Surprisingly, we found that the model discovers a compact edge-based construction for Sudoku geometry. Our results support explicit edge mechanisms as a promising architectural design, motivating broader evaluation.

</details>


### [253] [Mathematical Principles and Experimental Discoveries of the Emergence of Symbolic Patterns in Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06839)
*Quanshi Zhang,Qihan Ren,Siyu Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类广泛的人工神经网络（ANNs）的推理逻辑可以被重构成稀疏符号交互模式，并证明了导致这种符号模式涌现的两个数学判据，为ANNs的可解释性和泛化能力提供了新的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ANNs长期被视为黑盒模型，现有解释方法（如特征归因、可视化）大多是近似性的。作者质疑ANN复杂的推理逻辑能否被简洁、穷尽地解释为稀疏符号模式，以及这种符号模式的涌现是否反映自然规律而非偶然。

Method: 作者从理论上证明两个常见数学判据（跨任务隐含要求）会促使稀疏符号交互的涌现，并通过大量实验验证这两个判据在多样模型和任务中对大多数输入样本成立。同时通过样本间、模型间的可迁移性以及解释泛化能力来验证这些交互的忠实性。

Result: 实验证实了多数输入样本满足两个判据，符号交互具有较强的样本间和模型间可迁移性，并能有效解释ANNs的泛化能力。由于证明不依赖特定网络架构，该发现可能推广到其他黑盒系统。

Conclusion: 该研究为ANN的符号化解释提供了坚实的理论和实验基础，揭示了符号模式涌现的自然规律性，并提出了"通信学习"（communicative learning）范式，允许在符号模式层面直接检查和调优ANN的推理逻辑，补充了传统的端到端学习范式。

Abstract: Artificial Neural networks (ANNs) are often treated as black-box models, making explainability a central challenge in deep learning. Many engineering methods have been proposed to approximately explain the ANN from various perspectives, such as feature attribution and visualization. However, it remains a long-standing open question whether the complex inference logic of an ANN can be explained exhaustively and concisely as sparse symbolic patterns. This raises a deeper inquiry: does the emergence of symbolic patterns reflect a natural law rather than chance? Here, we show that across a broad class of ANNs trained on diverse tasks, their inference logic can indeed be reformulated as sparse symbolic interactions. We further prove that two common mathematical criteria, which are implicitly required across tasks, lead to the emergence of such sparse symbolic interactions. Empirical evidence confirms that the two criteria hold for the majority of input samples in diverse models. Furthermore, the faithfulness of these interactions is also demonstrated by their strong sample-to-sample and model-to-model transferability, as well as their ability to explain the overall generalization power of ANNs. Our theoretical analysis and extensive experiments provide a solid foundation for symbolic explanations of ANNs, and offer novel insights into the ANN's generalization power. Our findings also highlight the potential of communicative learning, a paradigm in which the inference logic of an ANN can be directly inspected and tuned at the level of symbolic patterns, thus complementing traditional end-to-end learning paradigm. Finally, the observed emergence of symbolic patterns in ANNs suggests that similar symbolic representations may also emerge in other types of black-box systems under certain conditions, because our proof does not depend on any specific ANN architecture.

</details>


### [254] [Bridging the Gap Between Hyperdimensional Computing and Kernel Methods via the Nyström Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06860)
*Quanling Zhao,Anthony Hitchcock Thomas,Ari Brin,Xiaofan Yu,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NysHD方法，利用Nyström核近似技术将用户定义的相似度函数转化为超维计算（HDC）中的高维映射，从而扩展HDC可处理的问题类型，在图和字符串数据集上分别平均提升11%和17%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HDC的有效性高度依赖原始数据到高维空间的映射方式，但现有编码方法限制了可处理的问题类型。作者希望通过引入核方法中成熟的设计来扩展HDC的应用范围。

Method: 基于Nyström核近似方法，提出一种将任意用户定义的正半定相似度函数转化为HDC高维映射的通用框架，使得核方法中丰富的相似度函数设计经验可以导入HDC体系。

Result: 与现有HDC编码方法相比，NysHD在图数据集上平均提升11%分类准确率，在字符串数据集上平均提升17%。

Conclusion: NysHD为HDC提供了一种基于核近似理论的新编码范式，成功架起了核方法与超维计算之间的桥梁，显著扩展了HDC的适用问题范围并提升了分类性能。

Abstract: Hyperdimensional computing (HDC) is an approach from the cognitive science literature for solving information processing tasks using data represented as high-dimensional random vectors. The technique has a rigorous mathematical backing, and is easy to implement in energy-efficient and highly parallel hardware like FPGAs and "processing-in-memory" architectures. The effectiveness of HDC in machine learning largely depends on how raw data is mapped to high-dimensional space. In this work, we propose NysHD, a new method for constructing this mapping that is based on the Nyström method from the literature on kernel approximation. Our approach provides a simple recipe to turn any user-defined positive-semidefinite similarity function into an equivalent mapping in HDC. There is a vast literature on the design of such functions for learning problems. Our approach provides a mechanism to import them into the HDC setting, expanding the types of problems that can be tackled using HDC. Empirical evaluation against existing HDC encoding methods shows that NysHD can achieve, on average, 11% and 17% better classification accuracy on graph and string datasets respectively.

</details>


### [255] [SkillAligner: Treating Retrieved Skills as Adaptable Drafts at Execution Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06880)
*Qinfeng Li,Dalin He,Yuntai Bao,Ying Yang,Ruoxi Chen,Xinyan Yu,Lizhou Liang,Ge Su,Wenqi Zhang,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillAligner，一种无需训练的运行时技能适应框架，通过一次性联合改编检索到的技能草稿，解决技能与执行之间的不匹配问题，提升语言智能体任务性能并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用技能虽然能提供可复用的程序性知识，但语义相关性并不保证执行实用性——检索到的技能可能包含与当前任务、执行环境或其他技能冲突的假设，即"技能-执行不匹配"问题。

Method: SkillAligner将检索到的技能视为可适应的草稿而非固定指令，在执行前进行一次性联合改编：针对任务需求特化有用的技能片段，将程序性假设与可用执行接口对齐，并通过解决依赖、冲突和冗余来组合生成的指导，最终整合为紧凑的执行指南供后续轨迹复用。

Result: 在多个智能体基准和模型骨干上的广泛实验表明，SkillAligner显著优于现有技能使用基线，减少了实例层面的技能诱导回归，并降低了总推理成本。

Conclusion: 将技能视为动态可适应的草稿而非固定指令，是解决技能-执行不匹配问题、提升语言智能体泛化性能的有效途径。

Abstract: General-purpose skills promise reusable procedural knowledge for language agents, yet semantic relevance does not guarantee execution utility: a retrieved skill may encode assumptions that conflict with the current task, execution environment, or other retrieved skills. We formalize this problem as the skill--execution misfit. To address it, we propose SkillAligner, a training-free execution-time skill adaptation framework that treats retrieved skills as adaptable drafts rather than fixed instructions. Before execution, SkillAligner performs a one-time joint adaptation that specializes useful skill fragments to task requirements, aligns their procedural assumptions with the available execution interface, and composes the resulting guidance by resolving dependencies, conflicts, and redundancy across skills. The adapted content is consolidated into a compact execution guide and reused throughout the subsequent trajectory. Extensive experiments across diverse agent benchmarks and model backbones show that SkillAligner substantially improves task performance over existing skill-use baselines, reduces skill-induced regressions at the instance level, and lowers total inference cost.

</details>


### [256] [PRISM: Principled Reference Identification for Schrodinger Bridge Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06893)
*Forouzan Fallah,Yezhou Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM，一个关于Schrödinger桥模型参考过程设计的理论框架，证明了在精确漂移和无限求解步数下所有可接受参考都能恢复真实后验，但有限计算预算下最优噪声谱与传感器破坏信息谱成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schrödinger桥模型中的参考过程（通常是白噪声）是启发式选择的，缺乏理论基础，作者希望建立一套系统的参考设计理论来指导实践。

Method: 作者刻画了保持精确可解性的时变高斯参考条件（瞬时协方差可交换），证明了"不可见性原理"，并在有限步预算下推导出闭合形式的有限步目标函数，证明最优噪声谱与传感器信息破坏谱P_k成正比。

Result: 高斯设置实验验证了预测的排序和闭合形式的损失下限；在FFHQ上，白噪声优于匹配参考，预注册研究排除ridge whitening解释，2x2机制研究将反转归因于真实图像的非高斯逐模式统计。

Conclusion: PRISM将参考设计从超参数搜索转变为高斯机制下的计算，并精确定位了真实图像突破该理论的位置，即非高斯逐模式统计。

Abstract: Schrödinger bridge models restore a clean signal from a degraded observation by following the conditional bridges of a reference process, yet this reference is chosen heuristically, typically white noise with a hand-tuned schedule. We develop PRISM, a theory of bridge reference design. We characterize the time-varying Gaussian references that remain exactly tractable with per-mode schedules: precisely those whose instantaneous covariances commute. We then prove an invisibility principle: with the exact drift and unlimited solver steps, every admissible reference recovers the true posterior. The choice of reference therefore matters only under finite computational resources. For a fixed step budget, we derive the finite-step objective in closed form and prove that every optimal noise spectrum is proportional to Pk, the spectrum of information destroyed by the sensor, with a mode-independent constant x*(T) = (2 ln T)^-1/2 (1 + o(1)). The analysis shows that noise color and temporal scheduling are interchangeable, and regularization provably shifts the optimal reference toward white noise. Experiments in Gaussian settings confirm the predicted orderings and the closed-form loss floors. On FFHQ, the distortion-- perception trade-off and spectral localization transfer, but white noise outperforms the matched reference; a pre-registered study that changes the training regime refutes ridge whitening as the explanation. A 2x2 mechanism study then traces the inversion to the non-Gaussian per-mode statistics of real images. PRISM turns reference design from a hyperparameter sweep into a calculation in the Gaussian regime, and locates exactly where real images break it.

</details>


### [257] [Recent advances in weakly supervised learning: New supervision paradigms, assumption relaxations, and practical solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06896)
*Wei Wang,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文讨论了弱监督学习的最新进展，涵盖置信度差异分类、互补标签学习和部分标签学习三个方向，提出了新的监督范式、更宽松的假设条件和公平的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的成功依赖于高质量标注数据，但在实际应用中往往难以满足。弱监督学习旨在利用不完整、不精确或不准确的监督信息训练准确模型，作者希望推动该领域在监督范式、假设条件和评估方法上的进步。

Method: 作者针对三个弱监督学习问题提出解决方案：1）提出置信度差异分类这一新的弱监督二分类问题及一致性求解方法；2）针对互补标签学习，基于比现有方法更宽松的数据生成假设提出多分类方法；3）为部分标签学习提出一个公平、真实的评估框架。

Result: 该工作涵盖了弱监督学习三个重要子问题的理论与实践方案，提出了新的问题设定、一致性算法以及更严谨的评估框架。

Conclusion: 弱监督学习是应对现实场景中数据标注困难的重要途径，未来需要在更宽松假设下不断推进一致性和实用性。

Abstract: Deep learning has achieved great success in recent years thanks to the availability of high-quality, well-annotated training data. However, this requirement is often not met in real-world applications. Weakly supervised learning aims to train an accurate model with incomplete, inexact, or inaccurate supervision. In this chapter, we will discuss recent advances in this field, including new supervision paradigms, relaxed assumptions, and practical solutions. First, we introduce a new weakly supervised binary classification problem called confidence-difference classification and propose consistent approaches to solve it. Next, we investigate complementary-label learning, a weakly supervised multi-class classification problem. Our proposed approaches are based on more relaxed assumptions about the data generation process than existing consistent approaches. Lastly, we present an evaluation framework for partial-label learning, another popular multi-class weakly supervised learning problem, in order to promote fair and realistic evaluation of algorithms in this field.

</details>


### [258] [MiCoPro: End-to-End Mixed Precision HW/SW Co-design with HW-aware Proxy Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06916)
*Zijun Jiang,Yangdi Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MiCo框架及其扩展MiCoPro，用于边缘AI应用中的混合精度量化（MPQ）探索与部署。MiCoPro引入硬件感知代理（HAP）模型，支持从PyTorch模型直接部署到裸机C代码，在BitFusion加速器和RISC-V处理器上实现高达40%的延迟降低，精度损失不到3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合精度量化（MPQ）方案搜索算法在灵活性和效率上受限，难以理解不同MPQ方案对后训练量化和量化感知训练的影响，且缺乏端到端的MPQ模型优化与部署框架。

Method: 提出MiCo框架，采用新颖的优化算法在严格延迟约束下搜索精度最优的量化配置；扩展为MiCoPro，引入硬件感知代理（HAP）模型提升预测精度和硬件通用性，通过目标特定延迟建模实现从PyTorch模型到裸机C代码的快速探索与直接部署。

Result: 在BitFusion加速器和SIMD扩展RISC-V处理器上验证了框架的通用性，实现高达40%的延迟减少，精度下降不超过3%。

Conclusion: MiCo/MiCoPro是一个全面的MPQ探索与部署框架，能够高效地平衡精度与延迟，支持多种硬件平台，为边缘AI应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Quantized Neural Networks~(QNN) with low-bitwidth data have proven promising in efficient storage and computation on edge devices. To mitigate accuracy degradation while maximizing speedup, layer-wise mixed-precision quantization~(MPQ) becomes a popular solution. However, existing algorithms for exploring MPQ schemes are limited in flexibility and efficiency. Comprehending the complex impacts of different MPQ schemes on post-training quantization and quantization-aware training results is a challenge for conventional methods. Furthermore, an end-to-end framework for the optimization and deployment of MPQ models is missing in existing work.
  To address these challenges, we propose the MiCo framework, a holistic MPQ exploration and deployment framework for edge AI applications. The framework adopts a novel optimization algorithm to search for accuracy-optimal quantization configurations under strict latency constraints. We further extended the framework to MiCoPro, which introduces a robust Hardware-Aware Proxy (HAP) model to enhance prediction accuracy and hardware versatility. By leveraging target-specific latency modeling, MiCoPro enables rapid exploration and direct deployment from PyTorch models to bare-metal C code. We demonstrate the versatility of our framework on both the BitFusion accelerator and SIMD-extended RISC-V processors, achieving up to 40\% of latency reduction with less than 3\% of accuracy drop.

</details>


### [259] [Walkable to Whom? Capturing Subjective Variability in Walkability Perception Using Multimodal Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06934)
*Moloud Damandeh,Meead Saberi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个用户条件化的多模态深度学习框架用于步行性感知评估，基于包含1,196名受访者、29,870条评级数据的澳大利亚人行道视角图像数据集，证明个体差异和图像视角对步行性评分有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有步行性感知研究常将多样化个体判断简化为聚合分数，假设感知是统一的；且多依赖车载街景图像，不能反映行人真实视觉体验，缺乏对个体差异的建模。

Method: 构建包含29,870条步行性评级的数据集（1,196名受访者），链接人行道视角图像与个体属性；提出用户条件化多模态深度学习框架，融合视觉特征与受访者级表征；并进行视角对比研究和用户条件化模型与图像仅基线的对比实验。

Result: 人行道视角图像评分显著高于匹配的街景图像；用户条件化模型相比图像仅基线，评级排名一致性提升65%（二次加权kappa从0.29提升至0.47）。

Conclusion: 评估者个体特征携带超越图像内容的预测信息，应摒弃聚合的、与观察者无关的步行性评分，转向能代表多样化用户的模型，以实现更包容的行人环境评估。

Abstract: Visual perception of walkability varies substantially across individuals, reflecting differences in personal characteristics, experiences, and preferences. Existing studies, however, often reduce these diverse judgements to aggregated scores, implicitly assuming uniform perception, and commonly rely on vehicle-mounted street-view imagery that does not reflect the pedestrian's visual experience. This paper introduces a dataset of 29,870 walkability ratings from 1,196 respondents, linking sidewalk-view imagery across urban, suburban, and regional Australian environments with individual rater attributes, and proposes the first user-conditioned multimodal deep learning framework for walkability perception, fusing visual features with respondent-level representations. A viewpoint-comparison study shows that sidewalk-view images receive significantly higher walkability ratings than matched street-view images, indicating that imagery source is a substantive design decision in perception surveys. The user-conditioned model improves rank agreement with observed ratings by 65% over an image-only baseline (quadratic weighted kappa 0.47 vs. 0.29), demonstrating that who is evaluating an environment carries predictive indication beyond image content alone. These findings support moving from aggregated, observer-independent walkability scores toward models that represent diverse users, enabling more inclusive assessment of pedestrian environments.

</details>


### [260] [ELMZip: Onboard Satellite Image Compression via Extreme Learning Machines for Efficient Downlink](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06942)
*Woojin Cho,Junghwan Park,Sangcheol Sim,Steve Andreas Immanuel,Junhyuk Heo,Darongsae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELMZip提出了一种基于极限学习机和域分解策略的星载图像压缩框架，通过将拟合过程转化为凸最小二乘问题，仅传输紧凑的输出权重，实现了无需反向传播的高效、免分辨率神经网络表示压缩，显著降低下行链路负载并保持高重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型卫星（如CubeSats）获取多光谱图像时面临下行链路瓶颈，传统压缩方法难以适应多波段、多分辨率数据的非线性统计特性，亟需高效的星载压缩方案。

Method: 提出ELMZip框架，基于极限学习机和域分解策略，使用随机特征单层网络将拟合过程建模为凸最小二乘问题，避免昂贵的反向传播计算；采用不对称传输协议，仅传输紧凑的输出权重而非完整网络参数。

Result: ELMZip在显著降低下行链路数据负载的同时保持高重建保真度，相比依赖迭代优化的传统神经表示方法，实现了更高的压缩效率，并支持即时图像重建。

Conclusion: ELMZip为资源受限平台提供了高效的星载图像压缩方案，能够最大化数据回传量，推动实时AI驱动的对地观测发展。

Abstract: The acquisition of multispectral imagery via small satellites (e.g., CubeSats) presents significant data downlink challenges due to high data volumes and restricted communication windows. While onboard image compression is critical to address this bottleneck, traditional methods often struggle to adapt to the nonlinear statistics of multi-band, multi-resolution data. To overcome these limitations, we propose ELMZip, a novel framework based on Extreme Learning Machines (ELM) and domain decomposition strategies for efficient, resolution-free onboard neural representation. ELMZip formulates the fitting process as a convex least-squares problem using random-feature single-layer networks, thereby eliminating the need for computationally expensive backpropagation. By adopting an asymmetric transmission protocol that sends only the compact output weights, the proposed method significantly reduces the downlink payload. Unlike previous neural representation approaches that rely on iterative optimization and require transmitting full network parameters, ELMZip achieves significant compression efficiency while maintaining high reconstruction fidelity. This capability enables immediate image reconstruction for analysis, allowing resource-constrained platforms to maximize data return and advancing real-time AI-powered Earth observation.

</details>


### [261] [A Rate Separation for Agnostic Direct Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06951)
*Mihir More,Aritra Das,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了agnostic PAC学习中直接和（direct sum）的学习曲线问题，证明单实例学习速率并不能决定直接和的学习速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hanneke、Moran和Waknine提出了一个问题：直接和C^r的agnostic PAC学习曲线如何依赖于单实例学习曲线以及参数r。本文旨在回答这个问题。

Method: 构造了两个具体的函数类进行对比：F类由两个常数二元函数组成，G类由零函数和恒等函数组成，两个类的agnostic学习曲线阶数均为n^{-1/2}。

Result: 通过这两个具有相同单实例学习速率的函数类，证明了单实例学习速率并不能决定直接和的学习速率。

Conclusion: 直接和的学习速率不仅仅由单实例学习速率决定，还受其他因素影响，这对理解PAC学习理论中的复合学习问题具有重要意义。

Abstract: Hanneke, Moran, and Waknine \cite{HannekeMoranWaknine2024} asked how the agnostic PAC learning curve of the direct sum $C^r$ depends on the single-instance learning curve $\epsagn(n\mid C)$ and on $r$. We show that the single-instance learning rate does not determine the direct-sum rate. Let $\F$ be the class of the two constant binary functions and let $\G$ consist of the zero function and the identity function. Both classes have agnostic learning curve of order $n^{-1/2}$.

</details>


### [262] [How Molecular Generative Models Organize Molecular Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06956)
*Raul Ortega-Ochoa,Tejs Vegge,Jens S. Bakander,Luis Mantilla Calderon,Alan Aspuru-Guzik,Tonio Buonassisi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究分子生成模型内部如何组织离散化学身份，通过"拉回"（pullback）机制探测生成同一对象的区域，发现模型内部存在固定的分区结构，由粗到细的边界分隔为分段常数区域，且该组织受多种因素影响，不能简单假设生成空间可导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅将生成模型作为采样器评估，对其隐藏空间内部如何组织化学身份知之甚少，缺乏对生成空间中化学可导航性的深入理解。

Method: 将分子身份显式化并通过生成过程进行拉回（pullback），探测生成同一对象的区域，从而暴露模型内部的化学身份分区结构。在三种分子生成架构上进行系统分析，考察表示类型、身份约定、解码器随机性和坐标度量等因素的影响。

Result: 发现分子生成模型的内部化学组织呈现分段常数区域，由粗到细的重复边界分隔；其组织依赖于探测的表示、身份约定、解码器随机性和坐标度量；训练期间局部化学组织趋于稳定，但每个邻域内不同分子身份的数量持续变化。

Conclusion: 分子生成空间的内部组织必须先被刻画而非假设，才能将其视为化学可导航的空间。

Abstract: Generative models for matter are often evaluated as samplers over output representations, and their latent spaces are commonly used as proxies for navigating chemical space. Much less is known about how these models internally arrange discrete chemical identities within those representations. We study this arrangement by making molecular identity explicit and pulling it back through the generative process. Through these pullbacks we probe the regions that generate the same object, exposing the trained model's internal repertoire: a fixed partition that determines which objects (novel or not) the model can produce.
  Across three molecular generative architectures, we find that this repertoire is arranged into piecewise-constant regions separated by recurring coarse-to-fine boundaries. Its organization depends on the representation probed, the identity convention, decoder stochasticity, and the metric used to compare coordinates. During training, local chemical organization stabilizes while the number of distinct molecular identities represented within each neighborhood continues to change. Internal organization must therefore be characterized, rather than assumed, before a generative space can be treated as chemically navigable.

</details>


### [263] [Density-aware Hierarchical Clustering Based on Element-Categorized Connection Subgraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06990)
*Yuning Yu,José Rodríguez-Piñeiro,Xuefeng Yin,Bin Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于元素分类连接子图的密度感知层次聚类方法（DHC-ECS），融合了层次聚类、密度聚类和图聚类，通过引入考虑距离、KNN连接子图中的元素分类、核密度估计和局部连通性的新型簇间相似度度量，在异构基准数据集上展示了优越的聚类精度和参数鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的层次聚类在计算簇间相似度时仅依赖成对距离，忽略了密度变化和图结构连通性，导致聚类效果不佳。

Method: 提出DHC-ECS方法，创新性地引入了一种簇间相似度度量，该度量不仅考虑距离，还结合了KNN连接子图中的元素分类、核密度估计以及子簇内的局部连通性，将层次聚类、密度聚类和图聚类有效融合。

Result: 在异构基准数据集上的大量评估表明，与基线方法（包括AChameleon、RNN-DBSCAN、McDPC和G-RMS）相比，DHC-ECS在聚类精度和参数鲁棒性方面表现出更优的整体性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明所提出的聚类算法通过利用局部密度和图结构连通性（即顶点和边的对偶性），对低维数据集具有巨大潜力，并可能确定内在阈值，减少对人工参数调节的依赖。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental data mining technique for pattern recognition through unsupervised learning. Among various clustering methods, hierarchical clustering, density-based clustering, and graph clustering stand out as representative approaches. For hierarchical clustering, it can be categorized into agglomerative and divisive modes to construct clusters in a recursive manner. The key aspect of both modes is the calculation of inter-cluster similarity, which determines whether to merge the sub-clusters into one cluster or divide a current cluster into sub-clusters. Traditionally, the similarity is derived from pairwise distances, often overlooking density variations and structural connectivity in graphs. To address this, we propose a density-aware hierarchical clustering method based on element-categorized connection subgraphs (DHC-ECS), which effectively integrates the hierarchical clustering, density-based clustering, and graph clustering. Particularly, a novel inter-cluster similarity metric is introduced that considers not only distances but also the element categorization in the KNN connection subgraphs, kernel density estimation, and local connectivity within sub-clusters. Extensive evaluations on heterogeneous benchmark datasets demonstrate that DHC-ECS exhibits superior overall performance in terms of clustering accuracy and parameter robustness compared with the baseline methods (including AChameleon, RNN-DBSCAN, McDPC, and G-RMS). The work indicates the great potential of the proposed clustering algorithm for low-dimensional datasets by leveraging local density and graph-structured connectivity (i.e., the duality of vertices and edges), as well as the possibility to determine an intrinsic threshold, reducing the reliance on manual parameter tuning.

</details>


### [264] [Beyond Foundation Models: Dimension-Aware Neural Architecture Search with Small-Data Representation Models for Cryocooler Lifetime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06993)
*Gregor Molan,Grafika Jati,Francesco Barchi,Andrea Acquaviva,Aljaž Osterman,Martin Molan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FSD-RM范式，针对工业小数据领域的遥测数据，采用容量可控的表征学习（结合CNN1D、LSTM、GRU、Transformer等编码器）配合维度感知神经架构搜索（NAS），在冷冻机遥测数据上实现了无需大规模预训练的竞争性预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练时间序列模型依赖丰富多样的数据，但工业和科学领域往往缺乏此类数据，因此需要一种适用于有限领域遥测数据的小数据表征学习替代方案。

Method: 提出了FSD-RM（小数据表征模型族）范式，采用容量可控的表征学习，使用成熟的编码器架构（CNN1D、LSTM、GRU、Transformer），在多元遥测数据上进行无监督训练，并集成到两阶段下游寿命预测流程中。通过维度感知神经架构搜索（NAS）联合优化模型容量和输入维度。

Result: 在冷冻机遥测数据上的实验表明，该方法在降低训练成本和模型复杂度的同时取得了具有竞争力的预测性能。

Conclusion: 当应用适当的归纳偏置和容量控制时，无需大规模预训练即可实现有效的表征学习，该工作贡献在于将成熟的表征学习技术与NAS驱动框架相结合，适配小数据场景。

Abstract: Large-scale pretrained time-series models achieve strong results through large-scale pretraining and task-agnostic representation learning, but they rely on abundant, diverse data that industrial and scientific domains often lack. We therefore propose the FSD-RM (Family of Small-Data Representation Models) paradigm as a practical alternative for limited, domain-specific telemetry. Rather than relying on large-scale pretraining, we focus on capacity-controlled representation learning using established encoder architectures (CNN1D, LSTM, GRU, Transformer), selected for their suitability in small-data settings and interpretability.
  These encoders are trained unsupervised on multivariate telemetry data and integrated into a two-stage pipeline for downstream lifetime prediction. To systematically examine architectural trade-offs under data constraints, we employ \textbf{dimension-aware neural architecture search (NAS)} to jointly optimize model capacity and input dimensionality.
  Experiments on cryocooler telemetry show that the proposed approach achieves competitive predictive performance while reducing training cost and model complexity. The contribution lies in combining established representation learning techniques within a coherent, NAS-driven framework tailored to small-data regimes, with explicitly defined parameter settings and design choices. The results indicate that effective representation learning can be achieved without large-scale pretraining when appropriate inductive bias and capacity control are applied.

</details>


### [265] [Every Cache Entry Earns Its Place: Global Allocation of Resolution and Coverage for KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07001)
*Haolin Tian,Yuzhe Liu,Tonghan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraceKV提出了一种全局KV缓存压缩方法，将压缩过程建模为固定缓存预算下的全局资源分配问题，通过原型树结构实现层、头、槽位间的缓存资源自由流动，无需额外训练，在32个设置中24个排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法依赖预定义的固定压缩规则，且通常基于token驱逐或合并单独开发，导致缓存资源无法跨层、跨头、跨上下文槽位自由流动，也无法联合分配以平衡局部分辨率和信息覆盖率。

Method: 提出GraceKV，将压缩过程形式化为固定缓存预算下的全局资源分配问题。将每个层-KV头-槽位组合视为原子单元并构建原型树：叶子节点对应token级KV条目，内部节点用单一原型压缩其子节点覆盖的KV空间；非重叠节点集合构成原子单元的表示。所有候选操作（新增树根扩展信息覆盖、分裂节点提升局部分辨率）在共享缓存预算下全局竞争，最终保留的节点构成压缩KV缓存。

Result: 在多种长上下文任务和压缩比下，GraceKV在32个设置中的24个排名第一，且在高达128倍压缩下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 全局预算分配在协调信息覆盖与局部分辨率方面的有效性得到验证，GraceKV无需训练且全流程在GPU上运行。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) process increasingly long contexts, KV cache storage and repeated access have become a major bottleneck. Existing KV cache compression methods rely on predefined, fixed compression rules and are typically developed around either token eviction or merging. As a result, cache resources can neither flow freely across layers, heads, and context slots, nor be jointly allocated to balance local resolution and information coverage. Therefore, we propose GraceKV, a global approach for the allocation of resolution and coverage in KV cache compression, and formulate the compression process as a global resource allocation problem under a fixed cache budget. GraceKV treats each layer-KV head-slot combination as an atomic unit and builds a prototype tree. Leaf nodes correspond to token-level KV entries, while each internal node uses a single prototype to compress the KV space covered by its children. A set of non-overlapping nodes in the tree forms the representation of an atomic unit. Adding the root of a new tree expands information coverage, whereas splitting a selected node improves local resolution. All candidate actions compete globally for a shared cache budget. Finally, the nodes retained across all trees form the compressed KV cache. This process adaptively determines the allocation of cache resources among atomic units globally and the balance between resolution and coverage. GraceKV requires no additional training, and the entire compression and inference process is performed on the GPU. Systematic experiments across diverse long-context tasks and compression ratios show that GraceKV ranks first in 24 of 32 settings and remains robust up to 128-fold compression. These results validate the effectiveness of global budget allocation in coordinating information coverage and local resolution.

</details>


### [266] [Hyperbolic Graph Embedders for Link Prediction and Topology Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07029)
*Robert Jankowski,Maksim Kitsak,Dorota Celińska-Kopczyńska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地基准测试了13种无监督双曲图嵌入方法，在统一协议下评估其在链路预测和拓扑重建任务上的表现，发现最大似然与表示学习方法整体表现最优，但没有任何方法在所有任务和结构场景中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲嵌入能够为复杂网络提供紧凑的几何表示，但来自机器学习、网络科学和算法学等多个领域的方法缺乏系统性的横向比较，研究者难以选择合适的方法。

Method: 在合成网络和实证网络上，采用统一的基准协议对13种无监督双曲图嵌入方法进行评估，涵盖缺失链路恢复和局部/全局网络结构保留两大任务维度，并按嵌入范式和学科来源进行分类分析。

Result: 最大似然方法和表示学习方法（包括混合变体）整体表现最强，但没有任何单一方法在所有任务和结构场景下占优。性能与嵌入范式的关联强于与学科来源的关联。

Conclusion: 研究识别了不同范式在不同网络结构条件下的优势和失败场景，为下游应用中的方法选择提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Hyperbolic embeddings provide compact geometric representations of complex networks in hyperbolic spaces, but systematic comparisons of methods developed in machine learning, network science, and algorithmics remain rare. We benchmark 13 unsupervised hyperbolic graph embedders under a unified protocol for link prediction and topology reconstruction on synthetic and empirical networks. The protocol captures both missing-link recovery and the preservation of local and global network structure. Maximum-likelihood and representation-learning-based approaches, including hybrid variants, achieve the strongest overall performance, although no method dominates across all tasks and structural regimes. Performance is more strongly associated with embedding paradigm than with disciplinary origin. We identify the network regimes in which different paradigms succeed or fail and provide practical guidance for method selection in downstream applications.

</details>


### [267] [Accounting Graph Transformer for Short-History Multi-KPI Forecasting in Small Businesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07037)
*Shrutendra Harsola,Vignesh Subrahmaniam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Accounting Graph Transformer（AGT）模型，用于小企业的联合12个月财务报表预测，涵盖13个KPI和71个月度账目序列，通过会计关系图上的类型化注意力机制实现多报表协调预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小企业通常仅有12-24个月的会计历史，但规划和风险管理需要跨财务报表的协调预测。现有方法难以同时预测多张财务报表中的多个关键指标。

Method: AGT将每个账目序列表示为掩码token，通过基于固定会计关系图的类型化注意力机制交换信息，池化目标特定上下文，并与门控的三个月近期路径融合。模型约5.3M参数，一次性生成156个对齐预测，无需公司特定微调。

Result: 在11,993个预测起点（来自1,060家未见公司）上，AGT达到样本加权KPI-macro MAE 0.6990，优于最强基线LightGBM的0.7378。在全部13个KPI上均优于LightGBM、TimeMixer和SOFTS。在7,094家额外未见公司（2025年1-5月）上，AGT获得0.7548 MAE，优于SOFTS的0.7694。

Conclusion: AGT通过利用会计领域结构（账目间关系图）显著提升联合财务报表预测精度，证明领域知情的注意力架构在小企业财务预测中优于通用基线模型，可为综合规划、流动性和营运资本分析提供统一的预测层。

Abstract: Small businesses often have only 12-24 months of accounting history, yet planning and risk workflows require coordinated forecasts across financial statements. We study joint 12-month forecasting of 13 income-statement, balance-sheet, cash-flow, and working-capital key performance indicators (KPIs) from 71 monthly ledger series. We introduce the Accounting Graph Transformer (AGT), which represents each ledger series as a masked token, exchanges information through typed attention on a fixed accounting-relation graph, pools target-specific context, and fuses it with a gated three-month recency path. Across 11,993 forecast origins from 1,060 unseen companies, AGT achieves sample-weighted KPI-macro mean absolute error (MAE) $0.6990 \pm 0.0013$ over three independent seeds, compared with $0.7378 \pm 0.0014$ for the strongest baseline, LightGBM. At the pre-specified seed 42, a paired company-clustered bootstrap gives a LightGBM-minus-AGT difference of 0.0395 with 95% confidence interval (CI) $[0.0350,0.0439]$. AGT is best on all 13 KPIs against LightGBM, TimeMixer, and SOFTS in the matched seed-42 comparison, while final-architecture ablations show that relational attention, accounting topology, and the recency path each improve validation and test accuracy. On 7,094 additional unseen companies with origins sampled from January-May 2025, AGT obtains 0.7548 MAE versus 0.7694 for SOFTS. A single 5.3M-parameter model produces 156 aligned forecasts without company-specific fitting, providing one forecasting layer for integrated planning, liquidity, and working-capital analysis.

</details>


### [268] [Beyond Isolation: Unlocking Reinforcement Learning Component Synergy for Sample-Efficient Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07086)
*Qi Zhao,Guozheng Ma,Yilun Kong,Lu Li,Haoyu Wang,Zilin Wang,Tiantian Zhang,Yuxing Wang,Jian Sha,Yongzhe Chang,Xueqian Wang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了强化学习系统中各算法组件的功能依赖关系，发现组件之间存在"协同或干扰"效应，并提出了ROSER框架来协调三个关键维度，在连续控制基准上实现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL系统设计需要同时考虑多个紧密耦合的因素，但各算法组件之间的功能依赖关系尚未被充分探索，不清楚它们之间是相互协同还是相互干扰。

Method: 首先进行系统性调查，发现不同组件的有效性存在显著的任务依赖性，简单堆叠SOTA技术并不一定带来性能提升，反而会引发复合非平稳性等问题。基于这些发现，提出ROSER框架，协调三个关键维度：基于模型的表示（Model-based Representation）、优化稳定性（Optimization Stability）和经验回放（Experience Replay）。

Result: 在多个连续控制基准上，ROSER持续优于vanilla基线，相比朴素堆叠方法取得了17.60%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 研究强调RL系统设计中需要采取整体性视角，而非孤立地改进单个组件，这为开发样本高效的智能体铺平了道路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning systems are significantly more complex than other machine learning paradigms due to inherent properties, causing RL system design to jointly account for many tightly coupled factors. Despite advances in individual algorithmic components, their functional interdependencies remain underexplored: do they exhibit mutual synergy or counterproductive interference? To bridge this gap, we conduct a systematic investigation and find that the efficacy of different components exhibits significant task-dependency, and naively stacking state-of-the-art techniques does not necessarily yield performance gains; instead, it often triggers emergent challenges, such as compounded non-stationarity. Building upon these findings, we distill a suite of actionable insights into the principled coordination of these components. Guided by these insights, we propose ROSER, an RL framework that coordinates three critical dimensions: Model-based Representation, Optimization Stability, and Experience Replay. Across diverse continuous-control benchmarks, ROSER consistently outperforms vanilla baselines and achieves 17.60% gains over naive stack. Our findings underscore the necessity of a holistic perspective in RL system design and paves the way for developing sample-efficient agents.

</details>


### [269] [Synthetic LiDAR Data Generation and Deterministic Downsampling for Point Cloud Classification on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07106)
*Niclas Meyer,Stefan Reitmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向Raspberry Pi 5低功耗嵌入式处理器的三维深度学习部署工作流，通过物理仿真构建合成LiDAR数据集以弥合CAD数据与真实传感器数据的差距，并集成特征驱动的关键点层（CPL）作为前端滤波器，在ARM Cortex-A76上实现约50 FPS的推理吞吐率和88.36%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维深度学习框架在低功耗嵌入式处理器上部署受到非结构化空间数据和资源密集型的距离排序算法的瓶颈限制，现有方法未充分解决真实场景下的传感器感知训练和边缘端延迟问题。

Method: 1) 使用物理仿真构建合成LiDAR数据集，以弥合干净CAD数据与真实传感器数据之间的现实差距；2) 集成一个隔离的、特征驱动的关键点层（CPL）作为前端滤波器，用于原始点云的确定性压缩。

Result: 预训练的CPL可将1024点云确定性压缩至40-60个唯一坐标；在ARM Cortex-A76处理器上，完整流水线实现约50 FPS的推理吞吐率，实例分类准确率达到88.36%。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了在边缘端实现确定性实时3D感知的可行性，同时强调了传感器感知训练对跨数据集泛化的重要性。

Abstract: Deploying three-dimensional deep learning frameworks to low-power embedded processors is bottlenecked by the unstructured nature of spatial data and the resource-intensive distance sorting algorithms often used before neural network inference. To address this gap, this paper presents a hardware-constrained workflow optimized for native execution on the Raspberry Pi 5. To account for the reality gap between noiseless, clean computer-aided design (CAD) datasets and real-world sensor data, we use physics-based simulation to construct a synthetic LiDAR dataset. Cross-dataset evaluations demonstrate a substantial drop in classification accuracy when networks trained on clean CAD data are evaluated on synthetic LiDAR sensor data, highlighting the critical need for sensor-aware training. To address the latency bottleneck of traditional geometric preprocessing on edge CPUs, we integrate an isolated, feature-driven Critical Points Layer (CPL) as a frontend filter. Our results show that the pretrained CPL deterministically compresses raw 1024-point clouds to a subset of 40 to 60 unique coordinates. When profiled on the ARM Cortex-A76 processor, the complete pipeline achieves an inference throughput of approximately 50 FPS while maintaining an instance classification accuracy of 88.36%, demonstrating the viability of deterministic real-time 3D perception at the edge.

</details>


### [270] [Modular TTT: Rethinking Test-Time Training as Composable Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07110)
*Bohao Tang,Zhen Qin,Yuqi Pan,Zheng Li,Pengfei Liu,Ya Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Modular TTT框架，将测试时训练（TTT）的内部学习器表示为有向无环图，把快速权重网络、损失函数、学习率、权重衰减和归一化作为显式设计维度，实现模块化组合和系统性消融实验，并基于发现训练出与Gated DeltaNet性能相当的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TTT变体通常各自硬编码实现，难以设计新的TTT方法，也难以隔离每个组件的作用，缺乏一个统一的模块化框架来系统研究各组件的影响。

Method: 提出Modular TTT框架，将内部学习器表示为有向无环图（DAG），暴露快速权重网络、损失函数、学习率、权重衰减和归一化等显式设计维度。框架自动将原语级的训练视图前向、训练视图反向和因果查询视图规则组合成完整的图级TTT计算，包括快速权重的状态转移。

Result: 系统消融发现：小学习率初始化、权重衰减和单层非线性可提升性能；MSE和内积损失表现相似；更深的快速权重网络和归一化会因产生过大的激活值而损害性能；残差连接和门控几乎没有可衡量的收益。基于这些发现训练的410M和1.45B参数模型（100B tokens）在训练损失和基准性能上与Gated DeltaNet相当。

Conclusion: Modular TTT提供了一个可复用的模块化框架，能够系统性地研究和组合TTT的各组件，并基于消融结果找到了有效的配置，达到了与主流序列模型（Gated DeltaNet）相当的性能。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) views sequence modeling as an online learning problem in which fast weights are updated by an internal learning rule. Despite the growing number of TTT variants, existing approaches typically hard-code each variant separately, which makes it difficult to design new TTT methods and to isolate the role of each component. To address this, we propose Modular TTT, a framework that represents the inner learner as a directed acyclic graph and exposes the fast-weight network, loss function, learning rate, weight decay, and normalization as explicit design dimensions. Modular TTT automatically composes primitive-level train-view forward, train-view backward, and causal query-view rules into the full graph-level TTT computation, including the fast-weight state transition. Using Modular TTT, we systematically ablate the components of TTT and find that small learning-rate initialization, weight decay, and a single-layer nonlinearity improve performance, while MSE and inner-product losses perform similarly. Deeper fast-weight networks and normalization tend to hurt performance because they induce excessively large activations, while residual connections and gating provide little measurable benefit. Guided by these findings, we train the best resulting variant as 410M- and 1.45B-parameter models on 100B tokens, and observe training loss and benchmark performance comparable to Gated DeltaNet.

</details>


### [271] [Online Conformal Prediction Beyond Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07139)
*Joar Skalse,Edoardo Pona,Osvaldo Simeone,Nicola Paoletti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OCPQ（带查询的在线共形预测）算法，在不获取部署预测反馈的情况下，通过部分监控博弈框架实现非独立同分布数据流上的不确定性量化，达到O(T^{2/3})的期望遗憾和β-O(T^{-1/3})的期望覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线共形预测（OCP）通常依赖先前部署预测的反馈来更新预测集，但在许多实际场景中无法获得这种反馈。本文旨在研究一个超越反馈的OCP设置，即每一轮只能选择输出预测集或查询真实标签，二者不可兼得，从而在无反馈条件下实现理论上有保障的不确定性量化。

Method: 将问题转化为一个部分监控博弈，其中预测动作不返回观测，单独的查询动作揭示标签。通过调整Cesa-Bianchi等人(2004)提出的label efficient forecaster算法来求解该博弈，设计了奖励函数以鼓励输出小预测集同时保证正确标签被覆盖的概率足够高。

Result: 对于任意黑盒分类器和任意长度为T的非独立同分布oblivious数据流，OCPQ算法达到O(T^{2/3})的期望遗憾、至少β-O(T^{-1/3})的期望覆盖率（β为用户定义参数），仅需查询期望T^{-1/3}比例的轮次。在真实数据集上的实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: OCPQ在无需部署预测集反馈的情况下，提供了与基于bandit的OCP方法相当的覆盖率保证，大幅减少了查询需求，为安全关键应用中无反馈场景下的不确定性量化提供了理论保障和实际可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is essential when deploying machine learning models in safety-critical applications. Online conformal prediction (OCP) provides theoretically principled uncertainty quantification for arbitrary black-box classifiers and non-i.i.d. data streams by constructing prediction sets that are guaranteed to contain the true label at a user-specified frequency. OCP usually updates prediction sets using feedback from previously deployed predictions. We instead study an OCP setting beyond feedback: on each round, the learner can either output a prediction set or query the correct label, but not both. Thus, no deployed prediction is ever evaluated directly. We reduce this problem to a partial monitoring game in which prediction actions return no observation and a separate query action reveals the label. The reward function is constructed in a way that encourages the learner to output small prediction sets while ensuring that the correct label is covered with a sufficiently high probability. To solve this game, we develop OCP with queries (OCPQ) by adapting the label efficient forecaster of Cesa-Bianchi, Lugosi, and Stoltz (2004) to our setting. For any black box classifier and any (non-i.i.d.) oblivious data stream of length $T$, OCPQ has $O(T^{2/3})$ expected regret and expected coverage at least $β-O(T^{-1/3})$ for a user-defined $β$, while querying only an expected $T^{-1/3}$ fraction of rounds. This provides coverage comparable to bandit-based OCP methods while requiring no feedback from deployed prediction sets. Experiments on real-world datasets further demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [272] [Interpretable reinforcement learning with decision-tree pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07151)
*Mark Leon Ringer,Michel Tokic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对决策树规则的剪枝方法，在保持任务性能的同时提升策略可解释性，并通过重执行策略来评估候选编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习策略难以检查，而将策略转换为决策树规则后仍可能过于复杂，难以被人类理解。因此需要一种剪枝过程来简化规则化策略，同时保持性能并使编辑可审计。

Method: 提出一个剪枝流程，定义了一组结构性和使用感知的编辑算子，通过重新执行策略来评估候选编辑，衡量回报和可解释性代理指标，从而实现从复杂到紧凑策略结构的转换。

Result: 在经典控制和MuJoCo基准测试中，剪枝轨迹显示出一致的可解释性提升，同时保持了高性能。

Conclusion: 该剪枝方法能够有效简化决策树形式的强化学习策略，在保证性能的前提下显著提升可解释性，并且编辑过程具有可审计性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning policies are difficult to inspect, but interpreting them is a prerequisite for trustworthiness. Converting a trained policy into explicit decision-tree rules improves transparency and the resulting artifacts often remain too complex for human understanding. We present a pruning process that simplifies such rule-based policies while preserving task performance and making edits to the policy auditable. The process defines a small set of structural and usage-aware operators and evaluates candidate edits by re-executing the policy to measure return and interpretability proxies. This exposes an transformation process from complex to compact policy structures. We investigate this approach on classic control and MuJoCo benchmarks, where pruning traces reveal consistent interpretability improvements while maintaining high performance.

</details>


### [273] [Machine Learning-Based Inter-Crystal Scatter Recovery for Ultra-High Resolution PET Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07155)
*Alexandre Bernier,Roger Lecomte,Jean-Baptiste Michaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种前馈神经网络方法，用于优化超高分辨率PET中晶体间散射(ICS)事件的恢复，通过推断首次康普顿相互作用的响应线，在保持亚毫米空间分辨率的同时将灵敏度提升70%-106%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UHR-PET中ICS事件对成像构成重大挑战，现有方法要么拒绝这些事件导致灵敏度降低，要么接受但使用次优定位算法导致图像分辨率下降。需要一种既能恢复ICS事件又不牺牲分辨率的方法。

Method: 采用前馈神经网络(feed forward neural network)推断首次康普顿相互作用所属的响应线(LOR)，从而优化ICS事件恢复。使用Monte Carlo模拟和基于LabPET-II的实验数据（包括临床前和脑UHR-PET扫描仪）进行验证。

Result: 与常规方法相比，灵敏度提升70%-106%，同时保持亚毫米空间可分辨性（可达1.6mm）。

Conclusion: 该ICS恢复方法是有效的解决方案，能够补偿小型像素化探测器在UHR-PET中较低的探测效率，在基本保持图像质量的同时实现更短的扫描时间和更低的辐射剂量。

Abstract: Inter-crystal scatter (ICS) events pose a significant challenge in ultrahigh- resolution positron emission tomography (UHR-PET), especially as detector crystals become smaller and their readouts increasingly segmented. Current approaches either reject these events, reducing sensitivity, or accept them with suboptimal positioning algorithms, degrading image resolution. We present a feed forward neural network to optimize ICS event recovery by inferring the line-of-response belonging to the first Compton interaction. Our approach was validated using both Monte Carlo simulations and experimental data from the fully pixelated LabPET-IIbased preclinical and brain UHR-PET scanners.Results demonstrate a 70% to 106% increase in sensitivity while preserving sub-millimeter spatial resolvability (down to 1.6 mm) compared to conventional methods. This ICS recovery approach is an effective solution that compensates for the lower detection efficiency of small, pixelated detectors in UHR-PET, enabling reduced scan times and lower radiation doses while largely preserving image quality.

</details>


### [274] [Trajectory-Relative Hindsight Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07371)
*Haoyu Zheng,Yun Zhu,Qing Wang,Wenqiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIAL框架，一种轨迹相对事后归纳蒸馏方法，通过统一的对齐打分协议，在智能体强化学习中将事后监督信号按回合合理分配，显著提升WebShop和ALFWorld上的任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体强化学习方法利用事后信号补充稀疏结果奖励，但一次完整轨迹可产生大量事后信号，如何在各决策回合间合理分配这些监督信号尚未解决。

Method: TRIAL在每个决策回合提取该决策实现结果的事后视角，并在普通与事后条件化上下文中评估同一响应；通过符号对数概率差确定token级别监督的方向和局部强度，回合级幅度在轨迹内联合归一化，使分配乘数的加权均值为1，在重分配密集监督的同时固定平均乘数。

Result: 在WebShop和ALFWorld上，TRIAL在8种骨干网络、环境和评估指标组合中全部优于GRPO，且在6种组合中达到六种方法中的最佳或并列最佳。在WebShop+Qwen3-1.7B上，成功率从56.4%提升至75.2%，任务分数从78.7%提升至85.7%。

Conclusion: 轨迹相对的回合分配机制为密集事后蒸馏带来显著额外收益，验证了合理分配事后监督信号对智能体强化学习的重要性。

Abstract: Recent agentic reinforcement learning methods use hindsight to complement sparse outcome rewards. However, a completed rollout can yield many such signals, leaving their appropriate allocation across turns unclear. We introduce TRIAL, a trajectory-relative hindsight distillation framework with a unified turn-aligned scoring protocol. For each decision turn, TRIAL extracts an outcome view of that decision's realized consequence and evaluates the same response under ordinary and hindsight-conditioned contexts. The signed log-probability gap determines the direction and local strength of token-level supervision, while turn-level magnitudes are normalized jointly over the realized trajectory. The resulting allocation multipliers have an eligible-token-weighted mean of one, redistributing dense supervision across turns while fixing its average multiplier. Experiments on WebShop and ALFWorld with different backbones show that TRIAL outperforms GRPO across all eight combinations of backbone, environment, and evaluation metric, while achieving the best or tied-best performance among six methods on six of them. On WebShop with Qwen3-1.7B, TRIAL improves the success rate from 56.4% to 75.2% and the task score from 78.7% to 85.7%. Controlled ablations further show that trajectory-relative turn allocation provides substantial gains beyond those of dense hindsight distillation alone.

</details>


### [275] [Capacity Confounds and Coverage Guarantees in Adaptive Sub-model Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07157)
*Alireza Moayedikia,Alicia Troncoso Lora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究子模型联邦学习中的自适应容量分配问题，发现基于客户端更新差异估计数据异质性的方法受设备容量混淆影响，且自适应分配存在隐藏失败模式（未覆盖参数停留在随机初始化），最终结论是参数覆盖比分配智能更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子模型联邦学习按设备资源分配容量，但自然下一步——根据服务器可观察的客户端更新估计数据异质性来分配容量——已被反复提出但未经验证。作者以HAS-FL为测试案例，系统检验这一步骤是否可行。

Method: 以HAS-FL为测试框架，使用两个修正后的估计器、多个数据集和多种随机种子，将更新差异估计与地面真值标签分布散度对比验证；引入匹配预算对照（随机分配与自适应分配比较）；覆盖保证机制以及均匀分配对照实验。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1)更新差异估计受设备容量支配而非数据异质性，控制容量后无数据信号残留；(2)自适应分配有隐藏失败模式——当所有客户端被限制在全宽以下时，未覆盖参数停留在随机初始化并逐渐腐蚀全局模型，覆盖保证可消除该失败；(3)匹配预算下，随机分配与自适应分配在图像基准上表现无差异，在文本基准上自适应策略最弱且消耗容量最多。

Conclusion: 子模型训练仍具价值（以平方级降低的成本容纳受约束客户端），但保护精度的是参数覆盖而非分配智能。自适应分配的表面收益来自容量预算和覆盖，未来设计需要可分离开容量效应的异质性信号。

Abstract: Sub-model federated learning lets resource-constrained clients train width-reduced versions of a global model, but existing methods allocate capacity by device resources alone. A natural next step, allocating capacity by each client's data heterogeneity as estimated from the updates the server already observes, has been repeatedly suggested. We ask whether that step is possible, using HAS-FL, an adaptive capacity-allocation framework, as a test case. Our findings are threefold. First, validated against ground-truth label-distribution divergence on reproducible partitions, update-divergence estimates of client heterogeneity are dominated by capacity rather than data: across two corrected estimators, multiple datasets, and all seeds, the estimates correlate strongly and negatively with device capacity, and no data signal remains once capacity is controlled for. This previously undocumented confound affects any method estimating client statistics from sub-model updates. Second, adaptive allocation has a hidden failure mode: when every client is capped below full width, the uncovered parameters stay at random initialization and progressively corrupt the global model. A simple coverage guarantee removes the failure and explains why uniform allocation collapses. Third, a matched-budget control settles what adaptivity contributes: random allocation to the same average budget performs no differently on both image benchmarks, and on the naturally partitioned text benchmark the adaptive policy is the weakest of the three strategies while consuming the most capacity. Sub-model training remains valuable because it admits constrained clients at quadratically reduced cost, but what protects accuracy is parameter coverage rather than allocation intelligence. Its apparent benefits come from capacity budgeting and coverage, and future designs need heterogeneity signals separable from capacity effects.

</details>


### [276] [Edge Sparsification via Temporal Forman-Ricci Curvature for Dynamic Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07158)
*Poupak Azad,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Kiarash Shamsi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRicci框架，将Forman-Ricci曲率扩展到有向加权时序图，通过边稀疏化实现大规模动态图学习的高效计算，在保留预测性能的同时减少约80%的边和56%的训练推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模时序图学习在稠密且快速变化的网络（如金融交易网络、通信系统和社会平台）中面临严峻的计算挑战，需要一种原则性的方案来降低计算负担同时保留关键时序结构信息。

Method: 提出基于网络曲率启发的边稀疏化框架TRicci，将经典Forman-Ricci曲率扩展到有向加权时序图，通过捕获结构支持、时间近因和局部交互竞争三个维度来指导边的剪枝与保留。

Result: 在9个交易网络和3个时序图基准数据集上，TRicci实现约80%的图稀疏化，端到端下游训练和推理时间平均减少55.94%，且预测性能无明显退化。

Conclusion: 时序曲率可作为大规模时序图学习的一种有原则的基础，在显著稀疏化条件下仍能保留具有预测价值的时序结构信息。

Abstract: Temporal graph learning has become essential for analyzing real-world systems whose interactions continuously evolve over time, including financial transaction networks, communication systems, and online social platforms. However, learning from large-scale temporal graphs remains computationally challenging when networks are dense and rapidly changing. To address this limitation, we propose a network-curvature-inspired edge sparsification framework for dynamic graph learning. Our proposed method, TRicci, extends classical Forman-Ricci curvature to directed weighted temporal graphs by capturing structural support, temporal recency, and local interaction competition.
  Experiments on 9 transaction networks and 3 temporal graph benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework preserves predictive performance across multiple graph-level prediction tasks. The results show that TRicci sparsifies temporal graphs by approximately 80% while reducing end-to-end downstream training and inference time by an average of 55.94%, without substantial degradation in predictive performance. Our findings suggest that temporal curvature can serve as a principled basis for scalable temporal graph learning by preserving predictive temporal-structural information under substantial sparsification.

</details>
